RB 2011 UiAS-2HnD

 قسمت اول ‎RB im‏ -+ بیانگر نوع برد‎Router iiss‏
‎Board‏
 ‏قسمت دوم شامل اعداد و حرف اختصاری +سری

 ساخت. تعداد درگاه ‎sla‏ فبزیکی و اسلات های بیسیم

 صورتزير
 * لادارای پورت‎USB‏
 ‎ o‏ ابیانگر قابلیت‎Power injection‏
 ‎QUES Ao‏ دهنده ارتقا بودن حافظه‎RAM‏
 ‎eo‏ 5 بیانگر پورت‎SFP‏
 ‏۰ 2 بیانگر باند فرکانسی ‎GHz‏ 2.4 یا 5 کهبیانگر
 باند فرکانسی ‎GHZ‏ 5است
‎High Powerful CPU SSH ۰‏
 ‎Sun eo‏ پشتیبانی از‎802.11n‏
 ‏* 0 بیانگر دو حلقه بودن آنتن بیسیم

 بنابراین اگر دستگاه از باند ۵ گیگاهرتز پشتیبانی میکرد:

 در نام مدل عبارت مربوط به آن مانند ‎SHN‏ وجودداشت.
 ‎OF‏ — گزینه‎@FannilandAcademy | @AliShiri_Ch B‏

 بررسی می‌شوند و اولین ‎Rule‏ مطابق با درخواست‎ol‏
 ‏خواهد شد. در ‎cpl‏ تست: 8۱62 ‎ple‏درخواست‌های
 0 از شبکه 192.168.1.0/24 را با دستور‎drop‏
 ‏مسدود می‌کند. بنابراین درخواست ‎Ping‏دستگاه‌هابی
 ‎ile‏ 192.168.1.50 قبل از رسیدن به ۷۱63متوقف
 می‌شود و ‎Rule3‏ نمی‌تواند ‎OF‏ را مجاز کند. اگر‎Rule2‏
 ‏حذف شود. درخواست ‎a Ping‏ ۸۱63 می‌رسد ومجاز
 خواهد شد. پس دستگاه 192.168.1.50 در حالتفعلی

      always working so hard.

       2) much

       3) a lot

       4)lots

       he--------see a doctor.

      21) has need

       2) was needing to

       3) has needing

       4) needed to

      hard and are honest enough.

       2) can overcome

       3) should overcame

       4) may overcame

Rule1: Chain: input | Src.Address: 192.168.1.100 | Protocol: TCP | Dst.port: 80 |Action: accept

Rule3: Chain: input | Protocol: ICMP | Action: accept

Rule4: Chain: forward | Action:accept
                                                                                                                                      .است روتر کردن Ping به قادر 192.168.1.50 دستگاه 

Part A: Grammar
Directions: Choose the word or phrase (1), (2), (3) or (4) that best completes each sentence. Then
mark the answer on your answer sheet.

31- Sarah says she feels sorry that she doesn’t have ……………. friends because she is always
   working so hard.
    1) many           2) much               3) a lot                4) lots
32- When Jim went to the hospital, a nurse took one look at his hand and told him he
  ……………. see a doctor.
    1) has need        2) was needing to      3) has needing         4) needed to
33- Dr. Johnson’s philosophy of life is that we ……………. our environment if we try hard and
   are honest enough.
    1) had overcome                          2) can overcome
    3) should overcame                       4) may overcame

Part B: Vocabulary
Directions: Choose the word or phrase (1), (2), (3) or (4) that best completes each sentence.
Then mark the answer on your answer sheet.

34- I thing Jake was a very wise person, especially because he always seemed to really
  ……………. everything before he spoke.
    1) agree           2) prefer              3) consider            4) behave
35- The manager told them that any ……………. activity – for which no money was paid – had
    to be outside regular working hours.
    1) vast             2) effective            3) nervous             4) voluntary
36- In many religions all over the world, it is believed that the birth of the prophet Jesus Christ
   was a ……………. .
    1) miracle         2) proportion          3) diversity            4) prevention
37- I care so much about my student’s learning that when I think I can’t improve it anymore, I
    get …………… .
    1) scientific        2) famous             3) impatient           4) endangered

Part C: Cloze test
Directions: Choose the word or phrase (1), (2), (3) or (4) that best completes each sentence.
Then mark the answer on your answer sheet.

Vapes, or e-cigarettes, contain chemicals that can cause serious health problems such as lung
damage. They also contain nicotine, which is highly addictive. It is safest to ……………. (38)
vaping entirely.  If you ……………. (39) vaping, quitting will have big health,  social, and
environmental benefits. Vaping and smoking are both unsafe and harmful ……………. (40) your
health at any age.
38- 1) avoid           2) attempt             3) attend              4) create
39- 1) have already started                    2) have already starting
    3) have started already                    4) have starting already
40- 1) at               2) on                  3) in                  4) to
